[ PART II -- SEC. 3 ( i) ] 


ररियन पेट्रोकेमिकल्स कॉर्पोरेशन लिमिटेड, महाराष्ट्र प्रेस कर कॉम्पलेक्स , 
विभाग, विलेपासे ( प . ) मुंबई , द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिये । 

और प्रतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाईनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन के 
सिए एतप्पाचन प्रनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित 
करला भागश्यक है । 

प्रतः प्रब पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाईप लाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के 
पधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम, 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा । 
की उपधारा ( 1 ) बारा प्रदत्त पाक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केंद्रीय 
सरकार में उसमें उपयोग का अधिकार प्रर्षित करने का प्रपमा प्राशय 


एप्तवारा घोषित किया है । बशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि में हिसषय कोई व्यक्ति, 
उस भूमि के नीचे पाईप लाईन बिछाने के लिए प्राक्षेप , सक्षम प्राधिकारी, 
इंडियन पेट्रोकेमिकल्स कॉर्पोरेशन लिमिटेड, महाराष्ट्र गस कर कॉम्पलेक्स, 
नगोठणे , तहसील रोहा, जिला रायगढ़ को इस अधिसूचना की तारीख 
से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा । 


__ और ऐसा पाक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति थिमिदिष्ट यह भी कयम 
करेगा कि क्या वह चाहता है कि उसकी सुनवाई व्यक्तिगत हो या किसी 
विधि व्यवसायी की मार्फत ।

में गांव मापगांव तहसील अलीबाग जिला रायगड से गांव बोरीस तहसील अलीबाग जिला रायगड तक पेट्रोलियम तेल अथवा नैसर्गिक 
गैस अथवा एप्ल्युअंट अथवा अन्य खनिज पदार्थों के परिवहन के लिए पाईप लाईन , इंडियन पेट्रो केमिकल्स कॉर्पोरेशन लिमिटेड 
महाराष्ट्र गैस क्रेकर कॉमपलेक्स विभाग, विलेपार्ले (प . ) मुंबई, द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिये ।

अत : प्रव पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाही लाईन ( मि में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 
50 ) की धारा 1 की उपधारा 1 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केंद्रीय मरकार ने उपमें उपयोग का अधिकार अजित 
करने का अपना प्राशय एतदद्वारा घोषित किया है । बशन कि उक्त भूमि में हिलबद्ध कोई घयक्ति , उस भूमि के नीचे पाईपलाईन 
बिछाने के लिए प्रयोग सक्षम प्राधिकारी, पिन पेट्रोकेमियास कॉर्पोरेशन लिमिटेड , महाराष्ट्र गस फर कॉम्पलेक्स , नागोठणा 
तहसील रोहा , जिला रायड को इस अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा । 

और मा श्राक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति विनिर्दिष्ट यह भी कथन करेगा कि क्या वह चाहता है कि , उसकी सुनवाई व्यक्तिशः 
हो या किसी विधि व्यवसायी की मार्फत ।

AWARD 


This is an application under section 334 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947, 


2 . Wlien the case is called out toduy, Mr, B . N . Lala , 
Advocato appears for the Opp. Party. Nopody appears for 
the applicant. Mr. Lala submits that in accordance with the 
direction of this Tribunal dated 8 - 9 - 1989 the notice dated 19th 
September , 1989 of this Tribunal was published in the news 
papers " Awaz" at Dhanbad on 13th October, 1989 and 
" Asansol Katha " at Asansol on 15th October, 1989. Mr, Lala 
files the copies of the said newspapers and presses for the 
dismissal of the application as the applicant has not appeared 
inspite of the substitute service of the notice . 


Serection of thisMr. Lula subopp . Party, 


3 . The application is pending from 1984 . All steps includ 
ing the substituto service of notice have been taken for 
onabling the applicant to appear but to no effect. In the 
circunstances the application under section 33A of the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 iy dismissed for default. 


This is my Award . 
Dated , Calcutta, 
The 17th November, 1989,

[ No. L-24012 (50)/ 83- D .IV - BIR( C .II )] 


___ 1. उक्त स्थापना में भम्बन्धित नियंकता केन्द्र सरकार 
के द्वारा समय - समय दिए गए निदेश के अनुसार जन अधि 
नियम की धारा 17 की धारा ( 3 ) के खंड ( क ) में 
उल्लिखित निरीक्षण के लिए मुविधाएं प्रदान करेगा और 
ऐसे निरीक्षण प्रभार की अदायगी प्रत्येक माह की समाप्ति 
के 15 दिन के अन्दर करेगा । 


2. नछूट प्राप्त स्थापनाओं के सम्बन्ध में उन अधि 
नियम और उनके अधीन मूजित उ47 स्कीम के अंतर्गत देश 
अंशदान के दर से स्थापना के भविष्य निधि नियमों के 
अन्तर्गत देय अंशदान का दर किनी समय भी कम न होगा । 


नई दिल्ली , 5 दिसम्बर , 1989 


का , आ . 3210. ----प नः म सम घाटग पटेल द्वासपोर्ट स 
( प्राइवेट लिमिटिड , 517 ई, पून बंगलौर रोड कोर 
और इसकी समस्त भारत स्पिन शाखाएं ( इनके आगे जहां 
कहीं भी उक्त स्थापना शब्द का प्रयोग हो इसने अभिप्राय 
जका स्थापना से है , ने कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि और प्रकीर्ण 
उपबंध अधिनियम , 1952 ( 1952 का 19 ) छपके आगे 
उक्त अधिनियम के नाम से निविष्ट को धारा 17 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के खंड ( क ) के अंतर्गत छूट प्राप्त करने के 
लिए आवेदन किया है । 

यह केन्द्र सरकार की राय में उन स्याना के कर्म 
चारियों के लिए तैयार किए गए भविष्य निधि नियमों में 
अंशदान की दर उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 में उल्लिखित 
कर्मचारी अंशदान की दर से कम नहीं है तथा इसके कर्म 
चारियों को मिलने वाले भविष्य निधि लाभ उक्न अधिनियम 
तथा कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि स्कीम , 1952 ( इसके आगे 
जहां कहीं भी स्कीम शब्द का प्रयोग किया गया है उसमे 
अभिप्राय उन स्कीम में है ) में उल्लिखित लाभों से किसी 
भी प्रकार से कम नहीं है जो इस वर्ग की स्थापनाओं में 
कार्यरत कर्मचारियों को उपलब्ध है । 


8. पेशनियों के मामले में छट प्रान स्थापना की स्कीम 
कर्मचारी भविस्य निधि स्कीम , 1952 से कम हितकर नहीं 
होगी । 

4. उक्त स्कीम में कोई भी संशोधन जो स्थापना के 
वर्तमान नियमों से अधिक लाभकारी है उन पर अपने आप 
लाग किया जाएगा । उक्त स्थापना के भविम निधि नियमों 
में कोई भी संशोधन क्षेत्रीय भविष्य निधि आयुक्त की पूर्व 
अनुमति के बगैर नहीं किया जाएगा और जहां किसी 
मंशोधन से उक्त स्थापना के कर्मचारियों के हित से प्रतिकल 
प्रभावी होने की सम्भावना है यहां अपनी अनुमति देने से 
पूर्व, क्षेत्रीय भविष्य निधि आयका , कर्मचारियों को अपने 
विचार प्रस्तुत करने का उचित अवसर देगा । 

6. यदि स्थापना की छूट न दी जाती तो वे सभी कर्म 
चारी जैसे उबत अधिनियम की धारा 2 ( च ) में निश्चित 
किया गया है ] जो सदस्य बनने के पात्र होते , सवस्म बनाए 
जाएंगे । 

6 . जहां एक कर्मचारी जो कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि 
( कानूनी ) या किसी अन्य छ : मान स्थापना का पहले से 
मदस्य है , को अपनी स्थापना में काम पर लगाया जाता है तो 
नियोक्ता उसे निधि का तुरन्त सदस्य बनाएगा और ऐसे 
कर्मचारी के पिछले नियोक्ता के पास भवि -य निधि लेखे भ 
संचयों को अंतरित कराने और उसके लेखे में जमा कराने 
की व्यवस्था करेगा । 


7. केन्द्रीन भविष्य निधि असक्त के द्वारा अथवा 
केन्द्रीय सरकार के द्वारा जैसे भी माम ना हो , समय- समय 
पर विए गए निदेशों के अनुसार भविष्य निधि के प्रबन्ध के 
लिए नियोक्ता न्यासी बोर्ड की स्थापना करेगा । 


भाग II --- खंड 3 ( ii ) ] 


भारत का राजपक्ष : दिसम्बर 23, 1989/ पौष 2, 1911 


3915 


8. भविष्य निधि , न्यागी बोई में निहित होगा जो अन्य 
बासों के होते हुए भविष्य निधि में आय के उचित लेखौं और 
भविष्य निधि में अदायगियों और उनकी अभिरक्षा में शेषों 
के लिए कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि संगठन के उत्तरदायी 
होगा । 

9 . न्यासी बोर्ड कम से कम 3 माह में एक बार बैठक 
करेंगे और केन्द्र सरकार द्वारा समय - समय पर जारी किए 
गार मार्ग निदेशों के अनुसार कार्य करेंगे । केन्द्रीय भविष्य 
निधि आय वप्त को अधिकार होगा कि वह किसी अन्य योग्य 
लेखा परीक्षक मे खातों की दुबारा लेखा परीक्षा कराए और 
ऐसे पुनः लेखा-परीक्षा के खर्च नियोक्ता वहन करेगा । 

10. न्यामी बोर्ड द्वारा रखे गए भविष्य निधि लेखे अर्हता 
प्राप्त निष्पक्ष चाटई अकाउन्टेन्ट द्वारा वार्षिक लेखा परीक्षा 
के अध्यधीन होंगे । जहां आवश्यक सममा जाए, केन्द्रीय 
भविष्य निधि आयुक्त को किनी अन्य अर्हता प्राप्त लेखा 
परीक्षक द्वारा लेखों की पुन : लेखा परीक्षा कराने का अधि 
कार होगा और इस पर हुआ व्यय नियोक्ता द्वारा वहन 
किया जाएगा । 

11 . प्रत्येक वर्ष स्थापना के लेखा परीक्षित तुलन -पत्र 
के माथ लेखापरीक्षित वार्षिक भविष्य निधि लेखों की एक 
प्रति वित्तीय वर्ष की समाप्ति के छ: माह के अन्दर क्षेत्रीय 
भविष्य निधि प्रायुक्त को प्रस्तुत की जाएगी । इस प्रयोजन 
के लिए भविष्य निधि का वितीय वर्ष पहली अप्रैल से 31 
मार्च तक होगा । 

12. नियोक्ता प्रतिमाह भविष्य निधि के देय अपने 
कर्मचारियों के अंशवानों की आगामी माह की 15 तारीख 
सक न्यामी बोर्ड को अंतरित कर देगा । अंशदामों की विलम्ब 
से प्रदायगी करने के लिए समान परिस्थितियों में निधोमा 
नकमानी देने का उसी प्रकार उतरदायो होगा जि प्रकार 
एक न छुट प्राप्त स्थापना उतरदायी होती है । । 

___ 13. न्यामी बोर्ड सरकार द्वारा ममय -समय दिए गए 
निदेशों के अनुसार निधि में जमा राशियों का निवेश करेगा । 
प्रतिभूतियां न्यासो बोर्ड के नाम पर प्राप्त की जाएगी और 
भारतीय रिजर्व बैंक के जना नियन्त्रण में अनमुचित बैंक 
की मभिरक्षा में रखा जाएगा । 

14 . सरकार के निदेशों के अनुमार निवेश न करने पर 
न्यामी बोर्ड अलग -अलग रूप में और एक माय केन्द्रीय 
भविष्य निधि आयुक्त या उसके प्रतिनिधियों द्वारा लगाए गए 
अधिक प्रभार का उतरदायी होगा । 

15. न्यानी बाई एक वर -व्यौरा रजिस्टर तैयार करेगा 
और व्याज और विमोचन प्राय का मनर पर बना सुनिश्चित 
करेगा । 

___ 16. जमा किए गए अंगदानों , निकाले गए और प्रत्येक 
कर्मचारी से संबंधित व्याज को दिखाने के लिए न्यासी बोर्ड 
विस्तत लेख तैयार करेगा । 
3524 GI/ 89 - 7 


___ 17. वित्तीय लेखा वर्ष की समाप्ति के छ: माह के अन्दर 
बोर्ड प्रत्येक कर्मचारी को वार्षिक लेखा विवरण जारी 
करेगा । 
____ 18. बोर्ड प्रत्येक कर्मचारी को वार्षिक लेखा विवरण के 
स्थान पर पासबुक जारी कर सकता है । ये पास- फुके कर्म 
चारियों की अभिरक्षा में रहेंगी और कर्मचारियों के प्रस्तुती 
करण पर बोर्ड के द्वारा इन्हें अद्यतन किया जाएगा । 
____ 19. लेखा वर्ष के पहले दिन प्रादि शष पर प्रत्येक कर्म 
चारी के लेख में ब्याज उस दर से जमा किया जाएगा जिसका 
न्यासी बोर्ड निर्णय करे परन्तु यह उक्त स्कीम के पैरा 60 
के अन्तर्गत केन्द्रीय सरकार द्वारा घोषित दर से कम नहीं 
होगा । 

20. यदि न्यासी बोर्ड केन्द्रीय सरकार द्वारा बोषित 
ब्याज की घर इस कराण से कि निवेश पर प्राय कम है या 
किसी अन्य कारण से प्रा करने में प्राई है तो इप कमो 
को नियोक्ता पूरा करेगा । 

21. नियोक्ता भविष्य निधि की चोरी के कारण 
लूट खसोट खयानत , गबन अथवा किसी अन्य कारण से हई 
हानि को पूरा करेगा । 

22. नियोक्ता और न्यालो बोर्ड , क्षेत्रीय भविष्य निधि 
आयुक्त को ऐमी विवरणियां प्रस्तुत करेगा जो समय -समय 
पर केन्द्रीय सरकार केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि प्रायन निर्धारित 
करें । 

23. उक्त स्कीम के पैरा 69 को शॉलो पर किसी 
कर्मचारी को निधि के सदस्य न रहने पर यदि स्थापना के 
भविष्य निधि नियमों में नियोक्ताओं के अंशदानों को जब्त 
करने की व्यवस्था है तो न्यासी बाई इस प्रकार जब्त की गई 
राशियों का अलग में लेखा तैयार करेगा और उसे ऐसे 
प्रयोजनों के लिए उनयोग करेगा जो केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि 
पायुक्त को पूर्व अनुमति मे सुनिश्चित किया गया हो । 

24. स्थापन के भविष्य निधि नियमों में निर्दिष्ट किसी 
बात के होते हुए भी यदि किसी व्यक्ति की सेवा निवृति 
होने के फलस्वरूप या किसी अन्य प्रतिष्ठान में नौकरी करने 
पर निधि की मदस्यता ममाप्त हो जाती है यह पता लगता 
है कि प्रतिष्ठान के भविष्य निधि नियमों के अन्तर्गत अंशदान 
को दर समपहरण की दर आदि मंविधिक योजना के अन्तर्गत दी 
गई दरी को तुलना मकन अन कल है तो अन्ना का वहन नियोकमा 
द्वाराकिया जाएगा । 

25 . नियोक्ता , भविष्य निधि के प्रशासन से संबंधित सभी 
खर्च जिममें लब्जों के रखरखाव रिटर्न प्रस्तुत किए जाने, राशियो 
का अन्तरण शामिल है , वहन करेगा । 
__ 26. नियोका मम चित प्राधिकारी द्वारा अनुमोदित निधिक 
नियमो की एक प्रति तथा जब भी कोई संशोधन होता है , उसको 
मुख्य बातों को कर्मचारियों के बह मन की भाषा में अनुवाद 
करके स्थापना के बोई पर लगाएगा ।

AWARD 
The Central Government in tho Ministry of I abour vide 
Order No . L -41012 | 14 86 - D . II ( B ) dated 22nd May, 1987 
has referred the following industrial dispute to this Tribunal 
for adjudication : 
" Whether the uction of the Railway Managemont of 

Northern Railway , Bikaner Division in terminating 
the services of Shri Vigyan Singh w . e .f, 30 - 5 -80 for 
alleged filing of wrong affldavit is justified ? If not , 

to what relief the workman cntitled to ?" . 
2 . Some of the undisputed facts are that the workinan 
Shri Vigyan Singh was appointed as a casual Labour Gang 
man on 31- 10- 1977 under the PWI Delhi Sarai Robilla 
Bikaner Division . The workman was given the CPC scale 
W .c . f. April, 1980 . 1 ho services of thc workman were termi 
nated w .e .f. 15 - 7 - 1980 without any charge sheet or enquiry 
or payment of any jetrenchment coinpensation . 

3 . The case of the workman is that he becamo entitled 
to the CPC scale of pay on completion of 120 days of get 
vice i.e . w .e. f. 21- 3 - 1978 but was actually given the regular 
pay scale from April , 1980 . Therefore, ho had to file an 
application under section 33- C ( 2 ) of the 1. D . Act beforo 
the Central Labour Court, New Delhi being LCA No. 168 ) 
81, for the recovery of arrears of wages for the period from 
21- 2 - 1978 to 21- 2 - 1980 on ground of acquisition of tem 
porary status and for the period from 14 - 6 - 1980 to 16 -6 - 1981 
un the ground of void ineffective and unjustified and illegal 
termination of liis service . The Labour Court vide order 
dated 19- 7 - 1985 allowed first part of the claim of the work 
man but turned down the second part of his clafon on the 
ground that this claim could not be examined under section 
33- C ( 2 ) of the I. D . Act. Hence the workman raised indus 
triul dispute unuer section 10 of the 1. D . Act. It is further 
submitted that the refusal of the employer to provide him 
with work on and from 14 -6 - 1980 without any formal en 
qury or show cause notico amounted to termination of 
services and us no retrenchment compensation was paid to 
him it was in contravention of section 25 - F of the I. D . 
Act. He denied that he had submitted any false affidavit 
regarding his age and stated that lic had subroittcd correct 
affidavit and the correct date of bis birth is 1 - 2 - 1953 but tho 
Management appeared to have been confused by the aff 
davit of Shri Sudhir Singlı who is not the real son of work 
man . Sudhir Singh happened to be the son of his clder 
brother Thakur Singh who expired since long when Sudhir 
Singh was only a small child . As per the custom , the work 
man maintained both the mother and son and that is why 
Sudhir Singh called him as his father and under this mis 
conception gave the name of workman as his father. This 
misconception and technical mistake of Sudhir Singh could 
not bind the workman and at any rate the mistakes affidavit 
of Sudhir Singh workman could not have been definitely 
used against the workinan, Hence the workman prayed that 
his terininution may be licld to be void abinition and non est 
in the eyes of law and he may be reinstated with continuity 
of service and with full back wages. 

4 . The Management filed a written statement which indi 
cates its confused state of mind as to what defence it shoulu 
take in this matter . On the one hand it was dealed that 
the applicant s sçrvices were terminated and at the same timp 
it has been submited that the applicant was removed from 
service because of his giving wrong date of birth as 1 -2 - 1953 
in the face of the fact that his son Sudhir Singh gave his 
date of birth as 6 - 8 -1958 meaning thereby that the workman 
was only 5 years 6 months and five days is older than his 
B011 which went to show that the date of birth of the appli 
cant was wrong (pray, it may be asked why the date of 
b rth of Şudhit Singh may not be taken as wrong) . It has 
further been stated that both the forkman and his son filed 


affidavit with regard to their date of birth . Thc workinen 
was issued one month notice dated 3 - 5 - 1980 in this behalf 
for giving wrong date of birth by him but he did not give 
any reply explanation to the said notice although it was 
duly received by him . Accordingly when no rcply /explana 
lion was received the Management had no alternative but 
to remove the applicant froni service from 15- 7 - 1980 after 
expiry of notice period . It has further been stated that the 
onus was on the workman to prove lis date of birth as 
1 - 2 - 1953 as correct, otherwise the Management had sulIi 
cient proof in the shape of two affidavits one given by 
Sudhir Kumar son of the workman and other by tlic work 
mun himsclf. As no reply came from tho workman the 
Management had no alternative but to remove the workman , 
from service and it was sufficient departmental enquiry in 
this case . It is only in the present industrial dispute thut the 
workman has come forward to say that Sudhir Singh is not 
his real son but his brother s son whereas Sudhir Singh has 
clearly stated in his affidavit that the workman is his 
father . 

5 . There can be no two opinions that the allegation that 
the workman had submitted wrong affidavit in support of 
bis age is a misconduct and also cause , a stigma on the 
workman . In that event, it was incumbent on the Manago 
ncnt to serve the workman with a charge sheet and to hold 
a domestic enquiry and also give the workman opportunity 
to defend himself. However, nothing of the sort was done . 
Both the affidavits of the workman and Sudhir Kumar were 
taken on their face value and an arbitrary inference drawn 
that tho affidavit furnished by the workono was falso . As 
has alrcady been romarked curlier , another inference could 
also be drawn that the affidavit furnislied by Sudhir Singh 
was incorrect. In any caso , both the affidavits could not to 
correct factually because the workman could not havo had a 
Bon at the age of 5 - 1 / 2 years. The proper course for the 
Management was to find out the truth of the matter and 
hold proper enquiry which it failed to do . It may also be 
noted that the affidavit of Sudhir Singli was got furnished 
by the workmen and , thereforo , the workman could not be 
accused of giving false age in his affidavit. The workman 
has examined WW - 2 Smt. Maya Devi who has described 
herself as the wife of Vigyan Singh workman . She has 
stated that Sudhir Singh is her real son born from the lions 
of Thakur Singh who was formerly her husband and after 
his death she got married to Vigyan Singh . Sudhir Singh 
was only 3 months old when Thakur Singh died . After the 
death of Thakur Singh Sudhir Singh was brought up by 
Vigyan Singh . The workman has stated that the said 
Thakur Singh was his real brother . There is no reason what 
soever to disbclieve the statement of Smt. Maya Devi. 
There is well known custom among jats of Punjab known as 
" Chadar Andazi according to which on the death of the 
elci brother the younger brother takes liis widow es his 
own wife and his children as his own children and looks after 
them . It appears that a similar custom is prevalent in dis 
trict Bhagalpur Bihar to which the workman and the said 
Sudhir Singh belong. It has been argued by tho Manage 
10¢nt that the DAR (Discipline & Appeal) rules were not 
applicable to the workman ard , therefore , no enquiry could 
be held . In the first instance even if the DAR rules were 
not applicable , the rules of natural instice were applicablo 
in tliis case . However , the contention of the Management 
that the DAR rules were not applicable to this workman are 
also incorroct. The paragraph 2511 of the Indian Railway 
Estublishment Manual provides as under : 

2511 . Rights and Pțivileges admissible to Casual La 

hour who are troated as temporary after completion 

of six months continuous services : - - 
( a ) Casual labour treated as temporary are entitled 

to all the rights and privilces admissible to tem 
porary railway servanty as laid down in Chapter 
XXIII of the Indian Railwave Establishment 
Manual. The rights and privileges admissible to 
such labour also include the benefits of the Dis 
cipline and Appeill Rules. Their service, prior 
to the date of completion of six month s conti 
nuous service will not , however , count for any 
purposes like reckoning of retirement benefits , 
seniority ctc . Such casual labourers will, also , bo 
allowed to carry forward the leave at their credit

not dond . Being not satisfied with tho explanation the work

9 . According to the management, the workman did not 
mention the ground of taking cave from 3 p . m , to 5 p . m . 
The ground is an after thought and the penalty of withholding 
of onc increment without cummulative effect for one year 
is just and proper , 

10 . On 9 - 2- 1984 ut about 2 pm , the workmun misbchaved 
with Dr. Prabha Bikrol and threatened her for dire conso 
quences during working hours. Therefore a Memorandum 
under Rule 16 of C . $. S . ( CCA ) Rules was issued and after 
consideration of his reply and evidence on record he was 
imposed minor penalty of censure . 

11 . During the Fainily Planning month the workman fail 
cd to perform the assigned duties and his performance was 
also poor, His short comings were informed on 8 - 4 - 1985 
but he did not show any improvement, hence a Memo under

sentation . Tlic disciplinary authority after considering the evi

12 . The caso of the workman wus also considered by the 
Regional Labour Commissioner , Jabalpur, The Factory is not 
aj industry and therefore this Court has no jurisdiction to 
try this case . Hence the reference should be rejectod . 

13 . The management in para 5 of its reply dated 27- 3 -87 
contended that the employees of Ordinanco Factories includ 
ing G . C . F . Jabalpur are Central Civil Services personnel and 
that tho cstablishment performing sovereign functions should 
he excluded from the purview of the term industry . The 
management has relied on case of Bangalore Water Supply 
and Sewerage Board Vs. A . Rajappa (AIR 1978 SC 969 ) . To 
my mind, this very caso decided by tho Hon blo Supreme 
Court in para 172 at page 973 has set the matter at rest 
and the Gun Carriage Factory is covered under the definition 
of industry as defined in Sec, 2 ( ) of the Industrial Disputes 
Act, 1947. The relevant portion of the judgment in case 
of Water Supply and Sewerage Bourd (supra ) is as under : 
“ 172 . One of the exception , carved out by the Court 

is in favour of activitios undertaken by the Govern 
ment in the exercise of its inalienable functions 
lunder the Constitution , call it regal, sovereign or 
any other name. I see no justification for excepting 
these categories of public utility activities from the 
definition of industry .. .. .. . .. .. .. .. .. . . . . . . .. .. . .. .. . . . 


a currency nole or strikes a coin , And yet, consi 
dering the nature of the activity , it is engaged in 

an industry when it does so ." 
The above juclgment of the Hon ble Supreme Court has fur 
ther been explained by the High Court of Madhya Pradesh 
at Jabalpur in M . P , No . 1969 /83 Rajendra Nadu Vs. Unioni 
of India and two others . No other point in this regard has 
been raised by the management. I, therefore , hold ibat the 
Gun Carriage Factory is an industry und Shri A . K Singhai 
is it workman under the I. D) , Act. 

14. Coming to the merits of the case the workman huis 
challenged the following orders under this reference : 

(i) Order dated 2 -3 - 1982 according to which the incre 

ment of the workman was withheld without cum 
mulative effect for one year in caso No. 281018 / 

82 /VO . (Ex. M / 1 ). 
(ii) Order dated 11- 4 - 1984 in case No . 442918 / 84 /VO 

according to which the workman was congurcu 

(Ex. W / 2 ). 
( iii) Order dated 26 - 9 - 1985 in casc No. 551118 / 85 / VO 

inposing the penalty of stoppage of one increments 
without cummulative effect for a poriod of two 

years. ( Ex . M / 9 ). 
15. So far the case No. 281018 / 82 /VO imposing penalty 
of withholding of one increment without cummulative effect 
for one year is concerned , it is based on the alleged miscon 
duct of the workman to the effect that despite his short 
Icave on 11 - 1 - 1982 from 3 p . m . to 5 p . m . having not been 
granted due to exigencies of his services he left the hospital 
without permission . This fact cau be gathered from Ex . 
M / 1 . According to the workman , he after making an ap 
pli aiien left the place of his duty on account of informa 
tion received by nim regarding the death of bis maternal 
grand -father and he had to attend the last uites of his mater 
nal grand - father . He has also pointed out that he was un 
duty right from 8 am , tu 3 p . m . and there was no exigency 
of his services when he left the hospital. According to bim , 
the only fault he committed was that when he had moved 
application , he had not given thc reasons for grant of leave 
and did not obtain the orders and had left the place of his 
duty, obviously without permission . 

16 . According to the management, the ground made out 
by the workman was an after thought. Whatever the fuct 
may be Ex, M / 2 discloscs that he had cxpressed regret for

17 , The workman has also filed Ex, W / 4 to show that the

tion for the period of absence in order to maintuin discip 
line in the hospital. While considering the facts of this case

harsh to a great extent. C . C . $ . ( C . C . A . ) Rules, Rule 1 
provides punishunent for such type of misconduct. In cases 
of such absence froni work , the leave sanctioning authority 
may order that the days on which work is not performed 
le treuted as " Dics Non " 1. c. they will neither count 115 
service nor be construed as break in service . It has further 
been expressed that this will be without prejudice to any 
Wher action that the competent authority might take against 
the persons resorting to such practices . 

18 . In the instunt case , it is not the averment made hy 
the management that the workman used to be absent froni 
duty off and on or this was his practice . 

19 . The workmun has explained the circumstunces in 
which he had left the place of his duty and the management 
in its reply has not shown that thero was any authority 
present to pass suitable orders on the application made by 
the workmat, That being so , fie penalty imposed in this 
case is liable to be set aside and is set aside, instead the 
period for which he was abgeot be converted into 1 / 2 day s 


Jf the water supply and scwerage schemes or fire 
fighting establishments rưln by a Municipality caji 
bo industries, so ought to be the manufacture of 
Coins and curreacy, army and ammunition and the 
winning of oil and uranium . The fact that these 
latter kinds of activities aro , or can only be , undor 
takco by the State does not furnishi any answer to 
the question whethor theso activities are industrjex . 
When undertaken by a private individual they are 
industries . Therefore , when undertaken by the State , 
they are industries. The outure of the activity is the 
determining factor and that does not change accorit . 
ing to who undertakey it. Itemy 8 , 11, 12 , 17 and 
18 of the First Schedulc reud wilh section 2 ( n ) (vi) 
of the Industrial Disputes Act render support to this 
view . These provisions which were described in 
Hospital Maziloor Sabha (AIR 1960 SC 610 ) as 
very significant at least show that , a conceivably , a 
Defence Establishments, a Mint or a Security Press 
can be an industry even though these activities are , 
ought to be and can only he undertaken by the State 
in tho discharge of its constitutional obligations or 
functions . The State docs not trade when it prints

2 . Shri Dhuri joined the erstwhile Vishwa Bharati 
Insurance Company Limited on 12 - 4 - 1953. At that 
time on the basis of his horoscope his birthdate was 
recorded as 24 - 4 - 1928 . After nationalisation of the 
Life Insurance business and establishment of the 
L .I. C . on 1st September, 1956 , the Corporation 
sought the bio -data of every employec in the pres 
cribed form and at that tiine Shri Dhuri gave bis 
birth date as 14 - 4 - 1929 . 


3. The L .I. C . of India , framed LIC (Staff ) Regu 
lations, 1960 under S . 49 of the Life Insurance Corpo 
ration of India Act, 1956 , defining the terms and 
conditions of service of the staff of the corporation . 
Regulation 19 of the said regulation provides for 
superannuation and retirement , Claule 1 of regula 
tion 19 stated that an employee helonging to class - III 
or class -IV shall retire on completion of age 60 .

PARTIES : 
Employer in relation to the managment of

Bombay. 


4 . In exercise of the powers conferred by Regula 
tion 4 , the chairman issued instructions, for vertifica 
tion of the date of birth of employees of the Corpo 
ration , which are known as Life Insurance Corpora 
tion of India ( Verification of the date of birtli of emp 
loyees ) Instructions , 1970 , As per thes ; instructions 
Shri Dhuri gave his date of birth as 15 - 11 - 1929 and 
produced his school Icaving certificate and a letter 
from the Tehsildar in proof of his claim . Tlie 
Senior Divisional Manager, Bombay returned these 
documents to Shri Dhuri and informed him by letter 
dated 6th March , 1978 that his date of birth was ad 
mitted by the Central Office as 24 -4 - 1928 , on the 
basis of his horoscope . 


and 


5 . According to the workman , the Corporation , 
having called upon him to tender proof of his date 
of birth was bound to accept as correct the date men 
tioned in the school leaving certificate issued by a re 
nowned school through which he passed his S .S . C . 
Examination and ignored the horoscopes. For two 
reasons this submission cannot be accepted . 


their workmen .

For the Management. -- Mr. A . W . Dharwadkar,

For the workmen . — Mr. A . S . Deo , General

tion . 
INDUSTRY : Insurance STATE : Maharashtra 
Bombay , da ed the 17th day of August, 1989 

AWARD 
The Central Government in exercise of the powers 
conferred by clause (d ) of sub - section ( 1 ) and sub 
section ( 2 - A ) of section 10 of the Industrial Dis 
3524 G1/89 -- 8 


6 . Firstly because it is not disputs that the date 
of birth was recorded in his service record as 
24 -4 - 1928 on the basis of the horoscope produced by 
him as proof of his age. It was never his case that 
the date of birth given in thc horoscope is wrong. 
Neither in his representations to the management nor 
in his sta ement of claim he averred that the date of 
birth given in the horoscope is wrong . He did not 
step in the witness box to say so on oath , 


7. Secondly horoscope is also recognised as a 
"listed document , on the basis of which the corpora 


3924 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : DECEMBER 23 , 1989 /PAUSA 2, 1911 


(PART II--Sec. 3 (11)] 


tion can validly record the date of tirth of an emp 
loyee . Clauses ( 1 ) and ( 2 ) of instruction no . 4 of 
the Life Insurance Corporation of India (verification 
of date of birth of employees ) instructions , 1970 which 
lay down how the age of an employee is to be entered 
into the record read as follows : 


" 1. The date of birth of every employee of the 

Corporation shall be entered in the staff 
record by an order duly made in accordance 
with the provisions of these instructions. 


either on the basis of any of the proofs 
specified in Entry Nos. 1, 2 or 3 of the First 
Schodule or after examining such proofs but 
accepting any other listed document, such 
entry shall be deemed to have been made 
under these Instructions by an order of the 

competent authority ." 
10 In the result therefore the action of the L .I. C . 
in accepting 24 - 4 - 1928 as the date of birth of Shri B . 
H . Dhuri was justified and the said workmen would 
not be entitled to any relief . Award accordingly .

भी धारा 17 के मनुसरण में , केन्द्रीय सरकार भारतीय जीवन बीमा निगम , 
अजमेर के प्रबन्धतरल के संबद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच, 
अनुबन्ध में निर्दिष्ट औद्योगिक विवाद में केन्द्रीय सरकार पायोगिया मधि

2 . No order for entry shall be made except on 

the basis of a listed document relating to 
the employee concerned and unless the docu 
ment demonstrably pertains to the employee 
and the authority passing the order is satis 
ficd that it is the most authentic, it has not 
been tempered with and it can be relied 
upon , that it shows the correct date of birth 
of the employee ." 


ter is ma of admissregister 
was bases Authority 
in con 


8 . The term listed document is defined by clause 
( i) of instruction no. 3 as meaning a document speci 
fied in the First Schedule of the instructions. An *ori 
ginal horoscope prepared soon after birth is men 
tioned at serial no . 9 of the First Sclicdule . As men 
tioned above it was never the case of Shri Dhuri that 
the horoscope tendered by him was not prepared soon 
after his birth and hence may not be correct. No 
doubt the school leaving certificate is also one of the 
listed documents. But the entry in the school Regis 
ter is made on the basis of the information supplied at 
the time of admission . There is nothing to show that 
the entry in the Register of the first school in which 
Shri Dhuri was admitted was based on any official 
record . The concerned Competent Authority of the 
Corporation was therefore perfectly justified in con 
firming the original date as recorded in the service 
record of Shri Dhuri, on the basis of the horoscope 
tendered by him . The fact that the corporation chose 
to verify the date of birth of Shri Dhuri docs not mean 
that the date of birth as recorded was wrong and 
the Corporation called upon Shri Dhuri to produce 
documentary proof to correct it . The verification was 
dune in resnect of every employee, as per the above 
mentioned instructions. It cannot tlierefore be con 
tended that as the workman was directed to produce 
documentary proof for verification of his age, the 
Corporation was bound to ignore the horoscope and 
prefer other listea document produced by the emp 
loyee .

the date of birth of the concerned workman was blank 
in Form B Register prepared by BCCL and that the 
relevant entry regarding the date of birth of the

cerned workman vide Ext . M - 2 dated 7 - 6 -80 , 


identity, card from his possession shows that his date 
of birth was recorded in it as 1 - 1 - 1927 and there 
fore he is not producing the identity card . It will 
appear that the concerned workman had not made 
any representation in writing prio : 10 May, 1986 
protesting regarding his wrong entry of age . The 
concerned workman had declared his age in Ext, M - 2 
on 7 -6 - 80 and had the said age been wrongly dec 
Jared by him the concerned workinan must have made 
a representation to the management soon thereafter 
stating that the date of birth as declared in Ext. M - 2 
before the age assessment committee was wrong . In 
absence of any protest it has to be accepted that the 
concerned workman had rightly declared his date of 
birth as 1 - 1 - 27 . 


In view of the facts, evidence and circumstances 
discussed above I ho d that the date of birth of the 
concerned workman was 1 - 1 - 1927 and that he has 
been rightly superannuated with effect froin 1 - 1 - 1987 
on completing his age of 60 years . 


The workman have not filed any document in sup 
port of the fact that his date of birth age was re 
corded as 1 - 1 - 1929 . Even no oral evidence has toen 
adducd in support of the assertion made on behall 
of the concerned workman . It will appear from the 
very evidence of the concerned workman WW - 1 that 
he has read upto Class VII in Bangladesh but he 
has not produced any School leaving certificate. He 
also does not remember his age when he had left. 
his studies in the school. He has stated that his 
age date of birth was recorded in Form B Register 
at the time of his appointment on his oral statement 


In the result , I hold that the action of the manage 
ment of Bhowra (North ) collicry of Ms. BCCL in 
retiring the concerned workman Shr . S . B . Chakra 
vorty , Asstt . Foreman from service with cffect from 
1 - 1 - 1987 is justified and consequently the concerned 
workman is entitled to no relief. 
This is my Award .

The hearing of the case commenced on 26 -7 -89 and on the 
same date the management submitted the aforesaid staterrent 
of facts before the Tribunal.

8 . The management had contended that this reference order 
has made in violation of the provisions of Sec. 10 of the 
Contract Labour (Regulation & Abolition ) Act, 1970 and 
the decision of Hon ble Supreme Court in Vegoils Pvt. Ltd . 
VS . The Workmen reported in 1971( 11) (LLJ. 567) . 


6 . The sponsoring union submitted its rejoinder on 
20 - 2 - 1989 . It appears that the management , after submission 
of the rejoinder of the union , contrived to make out a plea 
of inadvartance and made the unique discovery that it has 
got a Froth Flotation Plant which was installed in 1985 and 
thereafter proceeded to ramify and revamp its case obviously 
to deny and defeat the claim of the sponsoring union by 
stating as follows : 


Shri R . S . Murthy , learned Advocate for the management: 
has submitted that the demand of the sponsoring union is for 
abolition of contract labour with regard to the job of re - - 
moval of slurry / tailings as obtaining in Gidi Washery which 
can be done only by the appropriate Government under the 
provsions of Section 10 of the Contract Labour (Regulation 
& Abolition ) Act, 1970 . He has further stated that since the 
demand of the union is for abolition of contract labour sys 
tem in this job , the present reference is not maintainable . 


“ In the process of washing of coal some fine particles 

of washed coal get mixed up with the washing fluid 
solution and flow out of washery as a part of slurry . 
For preventing flow of such particles of coal, as a 
part of slurry , an additional Plant and Machienry 
in the form of Froth Flotation Plant has to be in 
stalled . While Gidi Washery started functioning in 
or about 1974 , the Froth Flotation Plant could be 
installed only in the year 1985 which is capable of 
not only recycling the Slurry for recovery of clean 
coal/ fine particles of coal for the purpose of use 
in the Steel Plants, but also for recovering such clean 
coal / fine particles from the Slurry which had been 
collected over the years and accumulated in huge 
stock running into several lakhs of tonnes. Due to 
technical difficulties and also deterioration of the 
quality of Slurry , which had accumulated over the 
years, the latter part of the machinery could not. 
be put into commission . The present position is that 
the recycling of slurry through Froth Flotation 
Plant is a regular part of operation of Gidi Washery 
and only coal rejects waste material ( tailings ) are 
flowing out into the Ponds through Froth Flotation 
Plant and are being collected and stacked by the 


The pleading of the sponsoring union has made it abundutly 
clear that the demand of the union is not for abolition of con 
tract system in this particular type of job and that the demand 
of the union is for regularisation of the workmen listed inx 
the reference as departmental workmen . Sri D . Mukherjee 
has also made this submission to counter the contention of 
Sri Murthy. 


9 . The pleading of the sponsoring union no where claims 
abolition of contract läbour system in removal of slurry / 
tailings as obtaining in Gidi Washery . It has simply dema 
nded regularisation of the concerned workmen as regular 
workmen of M / s. Central Coalfields Ltd . In the case reported 
in Vegoils Pvt. Ltd . VS. The Workmen 1971 ( 11)(LL ) . 567) 
the Hon ble Supreme Court has held that the demand for 
abolition of contract system does not fall within the purview 
of the Industrial Disputes Act. In the present case also the 
sponsoring union has not claimed for abolition of contract 
labour system and so the contention of Sri Murthy that the 


[ TTT 11 - 


3 ( ii ) ] 


HTET HT 179 : faF 23 , 1989; 979 11, 1911 


3933 


present reference is not maintainable within the frame-work 
of Industrial Disputes Act must founder on the ground . Ac 
cordngly , I hold that the present dispute is maintainable . 


10 . Before I pags on to the other issues, I consider it worti 
while to discuss one of the contentions of the management 
which fows from the present reference having been made by 
the appropriate Government Admittedly , the appropriate 
Government initially refused to refer the present dispute for 
adjudication to this Tribunal. Later , in terms of the order 
of Hon ble Patna High Court the appropriate Government 
was constrained to make this reference of industrial dispute . 
The management has stated that Hon ble Patna High Court 
gave direction to the appropriate Government to refer the 
Le for adjudication since the Counsel for the Central Go 
vernment failed to bring the decision of Hon ble Supreme 
Court in Vegoils Pvt . Ltd . VS . Thc Workmen to the notice 
of the Hon ble Court. This contention has also been reiterated 
h . the management in its rejoinder . 


13. The pleading of the management underlines the fact 
that Giddi Washery started functioning in or a boht 1974 . 
MW - 1 D , K . Saha , Superintending Engineer ( E & M ) of the 
Washery has also stated that the Washery started in or abou ! 
1974 und that between the period 1974 - 75 till the installation 
of Froth Flotation Plant the slurry was flowing out of the 
washery and used to be collected by man - power and stacked 
near the pond adjacant to the Washery. He had dmitted in 
cross -examination that the washing plant is situated in the 
precinct and premises of Giddi A Colliery and that Gidi A 
colliery is a non - coking colliery. According to him , originally 
the Washery was constructed for supplying non -coking coal 
to the Railways. But as the Rly , refused to take non- coking 
washed coal it was converted to a Washery of midium coklag 
coal and the function of the washery is to reduce the in 
purity of coking coal and also to reduce the ash contents . He 
has also stated that the Washery, froth Flotation Plant, 
Slurry Ponds and Stores are situated within one boundary 
having the same outlet and that there exist three slurry ponds 
which were constructed by the managenient , Slurry , according 
to this witness , is the mixture of stone cust. coal dust and 
sand etc , and before commission of Forth Flotation Plant 
slurry way bleeded out of the washery und deposited in the 
slurry ponds. Thus, the position is reached that Giddi 
Washery came into existence in or about 1974 and that func 
tion of the washery is to reduce inpurity ot the coking coal 
and also reduce ash contents and that slurry flow ng out of 
the washery and deposited in slurry ponlls was used to be 
collected by the man -power and stached near the pond ad 
jacant to the washery . He has fwther disclosed in his evidence 
that the concerned workmen have been regularly work ng in 
the slurry ponds. 


It is presence that the Hon ble Patna High Court was 
satisfied about thic merit of the case of the union and having 
coming to the conclusion that there exists no infirmity in the 
ca- c , it was pleased to direct thc approprate Government to 
make reference of industrial dispute for adjudication by the 
Industrial Tribunal, it is really unfortunate on the part of 
the management to suggest obliques that the Counsil for the 
Central Government failed to bring the aforesid decision of 
the Hon ble Supreme Court to the notice of the Hon ble High 
Court, Patna, as if that the Hon ble High Court was not 
angle of this decision or even if it was so aware it was not 
mindful of it or ignored it unwittingly This contention of the 
07. nagement is considered to be oſ reflection on the wishcom 
of the Judges of the Patna High Court. I consider that the 
contention of the management ils stated above is not only 
unfortunate but also deplorable as it tantamounts to he 
T HIT on on the wichdom and sagacity of the Judges of the 
Hon ble Patna High Court. 


11. The concerned workmen have claimed in the written 
stilement that they have been working within the precinct 
and premises of Gidi A Washery . The management has 
taken exception to it and has stated that the Central Coal 
filens Itd . has got no Washery hy the name of Gidi A 
Washery and that it has got a Washery hy the name ot 
Gidi Washery . The union has corrected this position in 
its rejoinder. Since the Central Coalfields Ltd . has got a 
Washery by the name of Gidi Washery I think that the 
management hay not been misled in the matter in any way 
and the pleading of the management eloquently testifies this 
position . 


The case of the sponsoring union is also that the concerned 
workmen have been working is slutry cleaning mazdoors in 
Giddi Washery since 1974 -75 . MW - 1 has admitted that all

hc has simply stated that out of 261 concerned workmen 13

only ono of them is dead and another workmen has left his 
employment to Join M / s . C . C . Ltd . Thus, from the evidence

the names of 2 workmen have been repeated twice. Thus, the

Workmen are left in the field . The contention of the manage 
ment in respect of other 11 workmen who are claimed by it 
to have died . cannot be accepted in view of the above facts 
and lacunae in the evidence of the managemeni. 


14 . The evidence of MW - 1 Dwrika Thakur discloses that 
all the concerned workmen have put in 270 - 200 days of at 
tendance in every calender year . He has further stated that 
even after installation of Froth Flotation Plant the concerned 
workmen including himself have been putting in Attendance 
for atleast 260 days in a calendar year. The management 
could not produce any evidence to disprove this evidence . 
That being so , I am constrained to conclude that the con 
cerned 259 workmen have been working in Gidi Washiery 
since 1974 and that they have been rendering continuous ser 
vice in the Washery ,

17 . The case of the sponsoring union is that slurry cleaning 
is a permanent nature of job . This has been disputed by 
ihe nianagement. But in 1982 the management arrived at an 
agreement with the union led by R . C . M . S . This agreement 
indicates that cleaning of slurry pond is a permanent nature 
of job (Ext. W - 17 ). The evidence discloses that the conter 
ned workmen havc been regularly working in the slurry / 
tailings ponds continuously even after the installation of Frih 
Flotation Plant in 1985 . This is indicative of the fact that tho 
job of slurry tailings cleaning is a permanent nature of jun 
Even highly placed responsible officer AS Regional LOL ,UT 
Commissioner ( C ) , Dhanbad (MW - 2 ) has stated that it 
is a matter for the Government to decide whether 70 
job of slurry cleaning is permanent nature of job or rota 
but, nevertheless, the job has come to stay . This indirectly 
means that the job has got permanency in character . WW - 1 
Eurika Thakur has also stated that the job of the concer 
ned workmen in the washery is of permanent nature . The 
management has stated in its petition that the process of 
collection and stacking of rejects is no longer necessary and

agement . But not a whit of evidence has been laid to sh : w 
that the management has been contemplating to discharge 
rejects / tailings to a suitable site. This being so , I cumc to 
the conclusion that the job of slurry / tail ngerejecte cleaning 
is of a permanent nature of job .

the purpose of use in the Steel Plant but also for recovery of 
such clean / fine particles frm the slurry which had been

Admittedly , the concerned workmen have been engaged in 
the slurry ponds of the management by a contractor, The 
contention of the management is that the rejects and waste 
D . terials (tailings ) have been flowing into the pond through 
Froth Flotation Plant and are being collected and stacked by 
the workers of a contractor . But the agreement with the

was awarded for cleaning of slurry and tailings from the 
tailings ponds and stacking it around the pond of Gidi

the contractor has copared for nianual extraction of slurry / 
ta .lings from the tajling pond and stacking the same on the 
Fast side of the Pond . Thus, the agreement produced by the 
management establish the facts that the contractor was en 
gag :: for manual extraction of slurry / tailings from slurry / 
lailings ponds of the Washery . Although , MW - 1D . K . Saha 
has endeavoured to state tliat duu at present the entire slurry 
blceding out of the washery is directed to the Froth Flotation 
Plant, he lias had to admit that only small portiun of 
slurry is left out but overwhelming portion is dicerted 
to the Froth Flotation Plunt. He has furthor admitted 
that slury bleeded out of the washery comes through two 
thickners . He hus further statcd that through one thickner 
slurry is diverted to the Froth Flotation Plant. The man 
Egement has attempted to establish a case that the other 
thickner remains idle . It has been disclosed by WW - 1 
in his cvidence that each thickner is provided with one 
pump and slurry goes direct from the washery to the two 
thickpers and that slurry from one of the two thickness 
directly goes into the slurry ponds and one -fourth of other 
siurry ocs to the Froth Flotation Plant and the three 
fourth of the rest goes to the slurry pond. This evidence 
of his has not heen displaced in cross- examination . MW - 2 
S . K Mukhopadhyay is the Regional Labour Commissioner 
( C ) posted at Dhanbad . He has stated that when he had 
occasion to visit Gidi Washery sometime in 1987 - 88 he 
saw that in spite of installation of Froth Flotation Plant 

lurry from the washery was being collected in the slurry 
ponds of the washery . Thus, from the evidence on re 
cord it is firmly established that not only tailings or re 
jects have been ficwing into slurry |tailings ponds of the 
washery , but also slurry has been flowing into these pondy. 

16 . In order to demolish the case of the sponsoring union 
the management at the time of hearing introduced a story 
that the tailings flowing out of the Froth Flotation Plant 
are of no use . Of course usefulness or otherwise of tail 
ings rejects have not been pleaded by the management in 
its pleadings . Anyway , MW - 4 Ajit Kumar Basak has been 
examined mainly to prove that the tajlings rejects are of no 
use . MW - 5 Uddho Pati), now posted as Manager , Quality 
Control, has been examined to prove that the tailings o re of 
no use. Photostat copy of letter indicating sending of slurry 
sample dated 4 - 12 - 86 ( Ext. M - 6 ) and analysis report ( Ext. 
M -7 ) have been laid in evidence by the management. MW - 5 
has stated that the management follows the standard pres 
cribed hv I. S . I with regard to taking of sample . But there 
is no vestire of evidence to indicate what is the standard 
proscribed hy I S . I, with regard to taking of samples. There is 
no evidence at all to show that samples were taken in accor 
dance with prescribed standard . 


18 . Admittedly , the management has got this job done by 
the concerned workmen through a contractor . Admittedly , 
wage bills (Ext. W - 10 ), profit- sharing bonus ( Ext. W - 4 ), sck 
leave ( Ext. W - 3 ) and bonus -sheet (Ext. W - 5 ) have been pre 
pared by the contractor. It also appears from the agreement 
that the contructor is liable to pay these benefits to the 
workmen . But nevertheless , it appears that the managemont 
has issued direction to the contractor no to employ substitute 
workers (Ext. W -6 ) . Then again the management has writen 
to the contractor directing him to submit details of the pur 
sons concerned who were involved in the Vakal Singhs murder 
case (Ext. W - 8 ). The management directed the contractor 10 
stop attendance of all workmen of slurry ponds who allegedly 
acted illegally by entering into the Gidi Washery Dispensary 
and man -handled the Compounder (Ext, W - 92 The manage 
ment has written to the contractor directing him to take de 
cessary action against the workmen who demonstrated in the 
office of the Project (Ext. W - 12 ). All these documents are 
S gest ve of the fact that the contractor is only name lender 
and the management is ultimately taking all actions frym 
behind , 


WW - 1 Dwrika Prasad has stated that they have been work 
ing for 8 hours a day and the management has been super 
vising their job and providing them work implements to per 
form their job . He has further stated that they have been 
getting wages as admissible to Group II cmployees ag rer 
N . C . W .AJII and that they are getting same benefits, such as, 
attendance bonus, annual bonus etc . at par with the perm3 
nent employees of the management. But they are not getting 
house allowance , L . T . C ., Conveyance allowance etc . He has 
empbatically stated that they have not seen the intermediary 
in person and so the question of his supervision and control 
over their job does not arise . The management has laid no 
evidence to prove specifically as to who supervise and contr1 
the job of the concerned workmen . If the principal employer 
ie, management does away with engagement of contractor , the 
concerned workmen will presumably be lose their source of 
earning . The concerned workmen render services for the 
business of the management and so the management is the real 
employer of the concerned workmen , The management has 
got control over the workers subsistence . skill and continued 
employment. In the circumstances , the management is held 
to be the real employer of the concerned workmen and the 
presence of an intermediary is nothing but a camoufledge or 
subterfuge adopted by the management. Thus , it is held that 
there exists relationship of employer and employec betweco 


In view of the fact that there is no pleading of the map 
agement that tajlings /rcjects are of no use and the evidence 
Jiscussed above I come to the conclusion that the case of 
the management that tajlings / rejects are of no use has been

THE SCHEDULE 
" Whether the emead of Koyla Ispat Mazdoor

It was for the union of the workman to establish 
the demand that the management of Kooridih colliery 
should regularise the concerned workman as Person 
nel Assistant as per the schedule of the order of refe 
rence . Although the workmen were represented by 
Shri D . Mukherjee in this case he left taking any step 
in the case on behalf of the workmen on the ground 
that he has no instruction and that no witness has 
turned up whom he could examine in the case . The 
learned Advocate appearing on behalf of the manage 
incnt did not examine anly witness submitting that it 
was for the workmen to establish their demand and as 
they have not adduced any evidence in the case , the 
management would not adduce any evidence and that 
the Award be made against the workmen . It will ap 
near from the schedule to the order of reference that 
the demand has to be established by the union and 
inview of the fact that the union did not examinc any 
witness to cstablish their demand , I hold that the 
union of workmen have failed lo establish their de 
mand . Accordinly I hold that the concerned workman 
is not entitled to he reularised as Personnel Assistont.

as follows:

submit as under :- -

vide their order No. 20012 10888 - 3 ( A ) 
dated 4 - 8 - 1988 have referred the above mat

not to what relief the workman is entitl 

ed ?" 
(2 ) That while the matter still pending before

cussed and negotiated this matter with a 
view to find out a mutually agreed solution . 
That as a result of this negotiation thc par 
ties have agreed to settle this matter on the 
following terms and conditions ; 


(1) The workman Sri Abdul Rajak Louder 

will be put back on the job of a driver 
with immediate effect on trial experimen 
tal basis for a period of six months, 


" Whether the demotion of Abdul Rajak from the 

post of driver to Miner |Loader by the Man 
agement of Bhaidee Colliery of M /s. Bharat

relief the workman is entitled to ? "

trial experimental period will be careful 
ly watched and the same is found to be 
satisfactory he will be absorbed as a te 
gular driver, 


2 . The dispute has been settled out of Court. A 
memorandum of settlement has been filed in Court, I 
have gone through the terms of settlement and I find 
them quite fair and reasonable . There is no rcason 
why an award should not be made on the basis of

of settlement, I accept it and make an award ac 
cordingly . The memorandum of settlement shall 
form part of the award .

put back ou his original job as Minor
